# Mapped Docs

> Documentation for the Mapped platform, API, connectors and gateways

## Guides

- [Guides](https://docs.mapped.com/guides.md): How to use the Mapped platform, its API, connectors and gateways.
- [Accruent Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/accruent-source.md): Import calendar events and room reservations
- [Ad Astra](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/ad-astra.md): Sync Ad-Astra room schedules and events into to analyze space usage and bookings efficiently
- [AiM Asset Management Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/aim-asset-management.md): Import assets and location information, and poll for work orders
- [AiM Asset Management Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/aim-asset-management-destination.md): Export Mapped Work Orders to AiM
- [Airly](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/airly.md): Provides air quality sensor data for environments around buildings.
- [Airthings](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/airthings.md): Track indoor air quality and radon levels in your building
- [ARD Access Control](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/ard-access-control.md): Track occupancy and events across your ARD system's doors and access readers
- [Aruba Central Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/aruba-central-source.md): Integrate with HPE Aruba Networking Central
- [Aruba Central Source Legacy](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/aruba-central-source-legacy.md): Ingest Aruba Central legacy WiFi occupancy and device status data into Mapped
- [AVEVA Insight](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/aveva-insight.md): Integrates AVEVA Insight Historian with Mapped.
- [Awair](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/awair.md): Access indoor air quality data from Awair sensors
- [Azure Event Hub - Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/azure-event-hub.md): Stream your Mapped data to Azure Event Hub
- [Beacon](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/beacon.md): Extract water meter analysis data
- [Butlr](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/butlr.md): Access live occupancy and people count data for your workspaces
- [CampusIQ Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/campusiq-source.md): Measure campus space occupancy
- [Cisco Spaces](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/cisco-spaces.md): Streaming occupancy counts into Mapped
- [Mapped for Niagara](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/connect-n4-install-usage.md): The Mapped for Niagara connector enables secure data ingestion from a Niagara Supervisor into Mapped.
- [Connector Entities](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/connector-entities.md): Stage entities from third-party connectors into Mapped
- [Coursedog](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/coursedog.md): Ingest campus room usage into the Mapped Graph
- [Crestron Fusion](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/crestron-fusion.md): Retrieve Crestron Fusion room availability data
- [Crestron XiO](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/crestron-xio.md): Enable room occupancy data
- [CSV Connector](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/csv-connector.md): Use the CSV Connector to import data to Mapped use CSV files
- [CSV Identities](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/csv-identities.md): Unify entities via ExternalIdentity values
- [Disruptive Technologies](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/disruptive-technologies.md): Provides location and occupancy services, along with sensor data like temp, humidity and Co2
- [Energy Star Portfolio Manager - Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/energy-star-portfolio-manager-destination.md): Energy Star Portfolio Manager Destination Connector allows you to send energy and water usage data to the Portfolio Manager application to generate metrics.
- [Energy Star Portfolio Manager - Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/energy-star-portfolio-manager-source.md): Energy Star Portfolio Manager allows you to retrieve meter data and EPA metrics available in the Portfolio Manager application.
- [EnergyCAP SmartAnalytics (Wattics)](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/energycap.md): Link water, electricity, gas usage and cost data to Mapped
- [EnOcean Sensors](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/enocean-sensors.md): Stream EnOcean environmental, occupancy, and control data into Mapped
- [FAMIS 360 Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/famis360-destination.md): Publish Mapped Work Orders into the FAMIS 360 CMMS
- [FAMIS 360 Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/famis360-source.md): Sync facilities data, assets, and work orders
- [Feenics Access Control](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/feenics.md): Access live occupancy and people count data for your workspaces
- [FSI CMS4 Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/fsi-cms4-destination.md): Export Work Orders into FSI CMS4
- [FSI CMS4 Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/fsi-cms4-source.md): Import location and assets for work order syncing
- [Monitoring Connectors with Grafana](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/grafana.md): How to set up Grafana to monitor Mapped connector metrics
- [Introduction to Connectors](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/intro-connectors.md): Instructions for adding or updating Connectors through Mapped Console.
- [Juniper Mist](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/juniper-mist.md): Access wireless network infrastructure data through the Juniper Mist cloud platform
- [Kaiterra](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/kaiterra.md): Connect your Kaiterra indoor air quality sensors to access to temperature, humidity, and air quality data.
- [Kastle](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/kastle.md): View access control event data pertaining to buildings, floors and spaces
- [Lenel S2 OnGuard 8.3](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/lenel-s2-onguard-8-3.md): Connects LenelS2 OnGuard access events to Mapped.
- [MACH Energy](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/mach-energy.md): Monitor energy demand and consumption across buildings. Electricity, and water utilities are covered.
- [MappedConnect Excel](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/mappedconnect-excel.md): Directly import entities and relationships sourced from outside a connector
- [Measurabl Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/measurabl-destination.md): Short imperative blurb
- [Microsoft Graph Source Connector](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/microsoft-graph-source-connector.md): Synchronize room calendar data from Microsoft 365
- [MQTT Destination Connector](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/mqtt-destination-connector.md): Deliver your Mapped data to any MQTT consumer
- [Nuvolo Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/nuvolo-destination.md): Publish Mapped Work Orders to Nuvolo CMMS
- [Nuvolo Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/nuvolo-source.md): Integrate Nuvolo CMMS with Mapped
- [OfficeSpace Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/officespace-destination.md): Export occupancy and presence data to OfficeSpace
- [OfficeSpace Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/officespace-source.md): Import building, floor, space, and workspace data from Office Space
- [OpenPath](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/openpath.md): View access control data pertaining to your buildings/floors/spaces
- [R-Zero](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/r-zero.md): Smart building platform providing occupancy detection solutions.
- [Redfish Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/redfish.md): Short imperative blurb
- [Robin](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/robin.md): Robin is a hybrid workplace experience platform that has features for desk reservations and spatial intelligence.
- [RTE France EcoMix](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/rte-france-ecomix.md): Connect buildings in France to national energy metrics
- [Soleran Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/soleran-destination.md): Sync work orders with the Soleran CMMS eAppTrack
- [Soleran Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/soleran-source.md): Import data and work orders from Soleran eAppTrack
- [Swift Sensors](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/swift-sensors.md): Monitor the status of mechanical assets
- [Tactic Room Reservations](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/tactic-room-reservations.md): Import room-scheduling data from Tactic
- [Tago.io](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/tago-io.md): IoT platform to connect your sensors for review and analytics
- [Utilivisor](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/utilivisor.md): Monitor energy demand and consumption across your buildings portfolio. Electricity, gas and water utilities are covered.
- [VergeSense](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/vergesense.md): Access live occupancy and people count data for your workspaces
- [Verkada](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/verkada.md): Link air quality data to Mapped
- [View Glass](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/view-glass.md): View Glass provides automated tinted windows for large buildings.
- [View Sense](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/view-sense.md): The View Sense connector allows you to connect to your View Sense AO, EQ, and PM devices to your Mapped graph
- [Vutility](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/vutility.md): Submetering to track energy metrics
- [WattTime](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/watttime.md): Grid emissions tracking
- [Weatherbit](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/weather.md): Virtual weather station allows you to retrieve weather data per building
- [WebTMA Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/web-tma-destination.md): Publish Mapped tickets to WebTMA
- [WebTMA Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/web-tma-source.md): Import Web TMA CMMS into Mapped
- [WellStat](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/wellstat.md): Access indoor air quality data from CO2 sensors, particulate matter sensors, etc.
- [Willow Events Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/willow-events-destination.md): Sync calendar events to Willow's event management system
- [Willow Import](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/willow-import.md): Import Willow twins into Mapped
- [Willow Ticket Destination](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/willow-ticket-destination.md): Push enriched Mapped data to the Willow Ticket Management system
- [Willow Ticket Source](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/willow-ticket-source.md): Ingest Willow ticket and service-request webhook events in real time into the Mapped graph with linked people, places, and asset relationships.
- [XY Sense](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/xy-sense.md): Integrate XY sensors for Occupancy and Indoor Air Quality
- [Yanzi](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/connectors/yanzi.md): Provides air quality sensor data for environments around buildings.
- [Overview](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/expert-center/expert-center-overview.md): Expert Center pairs an AI agent with targeted human expertise to turn a building's raw data into an enriched building model
- [BBMDs & Foreign Device Registration](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/gateways/bbmds-foreign-device-registration.md): Explains how the mapped gateway connects to networks

- [Retrieving Hostnames & IPs](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/gateways/retrieving-hostnames-ips.md): Api endpoint to retrieve Mapped's active hostnames and IP address

- [Filtering](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/platform/filtering.md): Walkthrough guide on how to use filtering

- [People](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/platform/people.md): People - detecting & tracking individuals as they move through a place
- [Place Management](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/platform/place-management.md): Create sites, buildings, floors and spaces using API calls
- [Places](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/platform/places.md): Places - the elements of your environment
- [Points](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/platform/points.md): Points represent the value of a device, like the temperature on a thermostat

- [Sandbox](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/platform/sandbox.md): How to use the sandbox and details about the data
- [Things](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/platform/things.md): Things - each individual device that generates data and sends it to Mapped
- [Users](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/platform/users.md): Adding, editing or removing Users in your Organization

- [Personal Access Tokens](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/tools/personal-access-token.md): Tokens that let your scripts and apps call the Mapped API, managed in the Mapped Console.
- [Webhooks](https://docs.mapped.com/guides/tools/webhooks.md): Send Mapped events to your own endpoints, managed in the Mapped Console.

## API Reference

- [API Reference](https://docs.mapped.com/api.md): Every query, mutation and type of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [@defer](https://docs.mapped.com/api/directives/defer.md): This directive allows results to be deferred during execution
- [@deprecated](https://docs.mapped.com/api/directives/deprecated.md): Marks the field, argument, input field or enum value as deprecated
- [@include](https://docs.mapped.com/api/directives/include.md): Directs the executor to include this field or fragment only when the `if` argument is true
- [@live](https://docs.mapped.com/api/directives/live.md): The directive creates Live Query based on the field, its subfields and variables.
- [@oneOf](https://docs.mapped.com/api/directives/one-of.md): Indicates an Input Object is a OneOf Input Object.
- [@skip](https://docs.mapped.com/api/directives/skip.md): Directs the executor to skip this field or fragment when the `if` argument is true.
- [@specifiedBy](https://docs.mapped.com/api/directives/specified-by.md): Exposes a URL that specifies the behaviour of this scalar.
- [assignGateway](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/assign-gateway.md): Used to assign a provisioned gateway to a building and/or spaces.
- [assignRole](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/assign-role.md): Used to assign a new acount role to a user (such as ADMIN); does not remove the previously assigned role.
- [cancelJob](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/cancel-job.md): Mutation cancelJob of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createBuilding](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-building.md): Used to create a new building for a specific site.
- [createConnector](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-connector.md): Currently in development; will be used to create a new Connector.
- [createConnectorAuth](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-connector-auth.md): Currently in development; will be used to create the auth details for a Connector, including token details.
- [createConnectorHost](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-connector-host.md): Mutation createConnectorHost of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createConnectorRequest](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-connector-request.md): Mutation createConnectorRequest of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createContributionRequest](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-contribution-request.md): Mutation createContributionRequest of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createCoordinateFrame](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-coordinate-frame.md): Mutation createCoordinateFrame of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createFloor](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-floor.md): Used to create a new floor for a specific building.
- [createFrameTransform](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-frame-transform.md): Mutation createFrameTransform of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createGraml](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-graml.md): Mutation createGraml of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createGramlVersion](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-graml-version.md): Mutation createGramlVersion of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createGrant](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-grant.md): Currently in development; will be used to add an access right grant to an external user so they can view specific organization place data (like granting building tenants the right to view data about a particular space).
- [createInvite](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-invite.md): Used to create a new invite to add a new user to the org.
- [createLiveQuery](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-live-query.md): Used to create a new live query.
- [createOrgRoleGroup](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-org-role-group.md): Mutation createOrgRoleGroup of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createOrgServiceAccount](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-org-service-account.md): Mutation createOrgServiceAccount of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createPersonalAccessToken](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-personal-access-token.md): Used to create a new personal access token, assuming the token you're using has the needed scope (PERSONALACCESSTOKEN_READWRITE or the admin scope PERSONALACCESSTOKEN_READWRITE_ALL).
- [createSecretOrgServiceAccount](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-secret-org-service-account.md): Mutation createSecretOrgServiceAccount of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createSite](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-site.md): Used to create a new site for the org.
- [createSpace](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-space.md): Used to create a new space for a floor in a building.
- [createSubBuilding](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-sub-building.md): Mutation createSubBuilding of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [createUser](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-user.md): Used to create a new user in the organization; only available for admins.
- [createWebhookTarget](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/create-webhook-target.md): Used to create a new webhook.
- [deleteBuilding](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-building.md): Used to delete an existing building.
- [deleteConnector](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-connector.md): Used to remove an existing Connector.
- [deleteConnectorAuth](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-connector-auth.md): Used to delete the auth settings for a specific Connector.
- [deleteConnectorHost](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-connector-host.md): Mutation deleteConnectorHost of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [deleteCoordinateFrame](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-coordinate-frame.md): Mutation deleteCoordinateFrame of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [deleteFloor](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-floor.md): Used to delete an existing floor in a building.
- [deleteFrameTransform](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-frame-transform.md): Mutation deleteFrameTransform of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [deleteGrant](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-grant.md): Currently in development; will be used to remove an access grant from an external user.
- [deleteInvite](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-invite.md): Used to delete an user invite.
- [deleteLiveQuery](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-live-query.md): Used to delete a live query.
- [deleteOrgRoleGroup](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-org-role-group.md): Mutation deleteOrgRoleGroup of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [deleteOrgServiceAccount](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-org-service-account.md): Mutation deleteOrgServiceAccount of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [deletePersonalAccessToken](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-personal-access-token.md): Used to delete an existing personal access token, assuming the token you're using has the needed scope (PERSONALACCESSTOKEN_READWRITE or the admin scope PERSONALACCESSTOKEN_READWRITE_ALL).
- [deleteSecretsOrgServiceAccount](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-secrets-org-service-account.md): Mutation deleteSecretsOrgServiceAccount of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [deleteSite](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-site.md): Used to delete a specific site from the org.
- [deleteSpace](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-space.md): Used to delete a specific space from a floor in a building.
- [deleteSubBuilding](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-sub-building.md): Mutation deleteSubBuilding of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [deleteWebhookReceiver](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-webhook-receiver.md): Mutation deleteWebhookReceiver of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [deleteWebhookTarget](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/delete-webhook-target.md): Used to delete a specific webhook.
- [enableWebhookTarget](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/enable-webhook-target.md): Used to enable or disable a webhook using a true / false boolean.
- [findNiagaraComponents](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/find-niagara-components.md): Mutation findNiagaraComponents of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [generateContainerSIDs](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/generate-container-si-ds.md): Used to generate containerized software gateway security identifiers.
- [getEdgeProfileByFingerprint](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/get-edge-profile-by-fingerprint.md): Mutation getEdgeProfileByFingerprint of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [getEdgeProfileFields](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/get-edge-profile-fields.md): Mutation getEdgeProfileFields of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [getGramlVersionDownloadUrl](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/get-graml-version-download-url.md): Mutation getGramlVersionDownloadUrl of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [getNiagaraComponentAncestors](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/get-niagara-component-ancestors.md): Mutation getNiagaraComponentAncestors of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [getNiagaraComponentDetails](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/get-niagara-component-details.md): Mutation getNiagaraComponentDetails of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [getNiagaraComponentSummary](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/get-niagara-component-summary.md): Mutation getNiagaraComponentSummary of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [importConnectorEntities](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/import-connector-entities.md): Mutation importConnectorEntities of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [listNiagaraChildComponents](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/list-niagara-child-components.md): Mutation listNiagaraChildComponents of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [mergeConnectorEntities](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/merge-connector-entities.md): Mutation mergeConnectorEntities of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [migrateConnectorEntities](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/migrate-connector-entities.md): Mutation migrateConnectorEntities of the Mapped GraphQL API.
- [pauseLiveQuery](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/pause-live-query.md): Used to pause a live query, so it stops actively monitoring for changes.
- [pingNiagara](https://docs.mapped.com/api/mutations/ping-niagara.md): Mutation pingNiagara of the Mapped GraphQL API.
